Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a cycles algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 586. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 410/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 586 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cycles structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 410. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
